"Father, when you get into the Path of Wisdom all things are revealed unto you in their Truth; and since I am only a boy of seventeen and know so much of this wonderful Creation so early in this lifefrom where did it come?" "You say a Silent Voice talks to you and tells you all things. What is this Silent Voice you talk about?" 

He smiles-"the Silent Voice is really the Soulmind, the Creator's portion of man, he gave man in the beginning; and said, 'I give thee a Soul-mind that will bring each material-mind into action and that Soul-mind can seek and have Wisdom and when you have found Wisdom all things shall be revealed unto thee.' So you see, as time goes on, so does the

Soul-mind. The body will die and be given back to the Earth from whence it came; but the Soul-mind goes on living, yes, even unto the end of time. It comes and goes as time goes on. If it is an accumulating Soul-mind, it keeps adding to its Wisdom and

so we have children born with this advanced Soulmind-Reincarnation. If we look for proofs of this, we can find enough to tell any of us this is true. We must keep our minds open and not let others lead us into the Path of Mysteries after death for mystery-there is none. The Voice of Wisdom tells us so. Does that satisfy you and answer your question? 

If you cannot see as I see, I would say, 'go through this life as you have been taught; then when you come again, the world will by that time have the 

Light of Truth and you will fall in line.' You see, it is only retarding your progress for this life, for the Voice says to me, 'every man must reach the place where the Creator saw him in the beginning and said, and I looked upon my work and it was good.' Since He said these words and meant them everyone of His Supreme Creation must reach a point of Wisdom to satisfy his Creator. He gave us that trust and we must fulfill it sooner or later; then why hold back when we can go forward. You will see even an advantage in that for he who does not play by the wayside, but keeps going on, will reap his reward by getting there before the others and can look back upon the others and even hold out a helping hand to those who are trying to reach him. So after all, no matter which way we look at it, we see life worthwhile and an incentive to get there." "You surely do clear up the mysteries of life, Son; but how can we reach that plane of life?" "Only by being your Natural Self as the Creator saw man in the beginning and in the end. He made us Natural; then we made ourselves Artificial. This is our doings. We put cloak after cloak on to hide

our true self from our neighbor; and the world is full of cloaked people but I think when I see the Creator look down upon us and see us wearing our cloaks, he has to smile and say, 'yes, I made you

Natural but you did not like the Naturalness I gave you so you created cloaks to hide yourselves. You may hide from your neighbor; but you cannot hide from me. Since I saw you Natural at the end of your world, you will have to dissolve some time-then why not now? Why hide yourself! Live in the

Light of Truth then life will be life indeed. But remember, I gave you a trust—please be big enough to fulfill that trust so my Creation will not have been a failure.' As we go on advancing, the small Silent

Voice keeps telling us what we are, from where we came, why we are here, where we go and where we go is of our own making. If we advance ourselves, we are making the Path of Life after this a more wonderful path to travel." 

The Mother says, "Son, you surprise me more and more, as you talk. I wonder if it is possible for a son of mine at so young an age to have found such Wisdom. I have been living in the belief for years that life does not end at the passing on of the material body. I could not see so great a Creator create a Soul for one short life and then put it away somewhere until the time of some Judgment Day; yet, as sure as I was, in all common sense, I did not speak of my thoughts to others for as soon as I did they thought and even said, I was a Lost Soul and would be punished for my lack of faith in the Light of the world today. I had to hold my peace and live my own life; but when you were born, I made up my mind when I looked upon you for the first

time that I was going to stand my ground and declare myself. I felt, as I looked upon you, that you had a beautiful Soul-mind. I watched you day by day and listened to the things you said; and the questions you asked convinced me more and more that you were the Reincarnation of a Great Mind.

I was a proud mother to have been given such a privilege. Today I look upon you and actually see my first thoughts come to life in all Truth. I remember a man who was different from any other man I had ever seen. He got on the train and sat behind us; and as he looked at you and you saw him you almost jumped out of my arms to get to him. When

I turned around to see the attraction, the man said, 'you have a most wonderful child. He has a beautiful Soul-mind; and if you bring him up right, he will become even greater than he was in his past life.' 

I looked at the man then more interestedly and as I did I saw him looking into the past and after a few minutes he seemed to come back and say to me, 'never let that child be banded by education or discipline. If you do, you will spoil a life, yes, a wonderful life and it would be a pity indeed. You have a wonderful trust-fill that trust, and someday you will be the proudest mother in the world.' He then said, 'we will meet again.' He got up and left and I never saw him again in the flesh; but so often I see him so plainly and he always seems to smile and say 'you are a wonderful mother.' I wonder if this is imagination or if he does come in reality. There is something strange about it. I have seen thousands of men and have had to look at them for different lengths of time; but after they have gone, I never see them even if I try to picture them; but this man I saw for

not more than five minutes; yet I see him so often just as I saw him in the train. He does not always say the same thing. He has a few words to say to me every time and they are always so wonderful I never can forget. The day before you came, I was standing in the kitchen when I felt something, I turned and there was my friend of the train standing beside me. When I looked at him, he seemed to say, 'what a treasure was given unto you. Value it as a true mother should.' I wonder who the man can be?" 

Then the young man says, "Mother, how old was 

I when we saw this man on the train?" "I do not think you were two years old." "I have a mental picture of some man; and that picture comes to me when I am drifting away to get in touch with the Silent Voice of Wisdom; and he seems to be the greater part of my life." "Son, can you describe this mental picture?"

He does and his mother is amazed. It is the same person whom she has seen. "I wonder if we will meet him again?" "Well, he said we would; but whether he meant in the flesh or the mental vision, I do not know. I hope we do some day."

Then his friend or Soul-mind Mother who has not spoken up to this time, says, "do you not think a little walk down town would do us good? Suppose we all go together."

They all arose, dressed and left and as they were going out of the door the mother turns to her and says, "why do you want to go out? I was enjoying his conversation so much." 

Then the friend of our Master-mind whispers.

"Never try to take a quart of water out of a pint jug. He is but seventeen, you know." 

The mother smiles and says, "Yes, I chose well, when I chose you."

They spend the night with the masses and came home late and retire. 

A glimpse into the world 

Will change our thoughts awhile; 

But when tomorrow comes, We will live in Truth once more.

CHAPTER XI THE RECOGNITION OF LOVE THROUGH MUSIC 

Once more he goes; but just to gaze, Upon the wheel of life;

Where once he refused to be a cog 

That he might know real life. 

Next morning, as they sit at breakfast in a general conversation, the father asks his son if he wants to go to his office with him. "Why, yes, I want to," so they set out for their daily routine in the wheel of industry. The father in his natural state and the boy with an anticipation of having once more a glimpse of life's struggle to live. His life is taken care of financially, he feels assured, so he can look from the standpoint of an observer of the Human Machine. 

They arrive at the office where he left six months ago. He goes the rounds of the office and speaks to each one of his father's employees. Some have struggled with the firm for years; yet they sit in the same chair, go over the same figures on the same paper, seemingly; and they seem to be satisfied with life. How little do they know! How limitless is the mind of man! As he looks upon them, he feels a sort of sympathy for them and wonders how they can be satisfied with life. The reason of this seems to be answered by the Silent Voice-"they know no better, they are like the horse, they eat, they labor for their food and since they know no more they are satisfied. Like the sheep in the field and the beast

LOVE THROUGH MUSIC 

77 and everything that has a routine forced upon them." 

The Voice says again to him "Never get into a groove of life you cannot or are not big enough to get out of. Never form a daily habit. Let every day bring its own habit and you will fit in anywhere, no matter where it may be or whom you may meet -whether it be a man cleaning the street-crossing or a man in a position of what the world calls big, you will realize as you travel and meet people that it is neither the cloak nor the position that makes the man; but the Soul-mind that dwells within the body of the man. Look around your father's office and at his employees-has anyone of them a higher position than the other? No, for if there was nothing for them to do, they would all become equal-all men out of employment. There is no distinction only in the eyes of men of the material world." 

He was sitting quietly alone and hearing these things. His father enters and, in his hand, holds a piece of paper. He hands it to his son to read and this is what he sees, "business for sale. anyone who would see the possibility. and So for information." 

Price to suit 

Apply to So

The son reads it and says to his father, "I am more than pleased with your decision. I hope it will do as much for you as your decision did for me eleven years ago."

The father replies, "I know now that was the greatest work I have done in my life. Without that day, my life would have been a failure. Son, I am proud of you-not for what you have done for me; but for what you are and for what you are going to be. You have surely lifted the mist from my life. 

I feel as if I had been born again."

The son says, "if you feel that way, my life is worthwhile already; but I see a path ahead and there seems to be no end to it. The further I can see the more beautiful a picture it is for it is a life with a Light ahead and that Light is so wonderful you who have gotten the first glimpse of it can never get away from the longing and rushing that you could travel faster and faster so we can see more of the meaning of this wonderful Light. Life, you know, is one never-ending journey through time; and time has no end. The wisdom you can find in time is limitless as time. Man never stops, he must go on and once he sees and understands this Truth, he takes interest in walking the Path. Dad, I hope as you live on in the future you may see life in its True

Light, and gain by the living of this life. You will be assured there is no limit to the height that can be reached by man. Maybe you will think I am trying to exalt myself before you, and I being so young; but, no, as the Silent Voice speaks so do I. I feel, many times, when I am alone, I know so little. How small I am! Yes, I realize I have to take these steps with care. I know enough to leave it to the Silent

Voice of the Soul-mind. I know as my material brain matures so will the Soul-mind speak bigger thoughts, give me more beautiful pictures, make the mysteries of life clearer to me and put me on the mountain of knowledge. I will be satisfied to want and see these things revealed unto me as they want to reveal themselves." "Son, you speak so wonderful, so deep that I am afraid you are far above your father. It makes me wonder what I have been doing to have lived so long and know so little."

LOVE THROUGH MUSIC 

79 "Well, Dad, we are not all born with an advanced 

Soul-mind. You know, it is not really your fault; but since you see the truth, from now on if you fail to advance, it will be you. Yes, only you. I would say, 'hold on to that which I have given you; but go after more in your own way. Do not let anyone carry you through. Go through of yourself.' 999 "Son, if it be possible, I will do as you say. It is twelve o'clock? Can it be that the morning has gone so quickly; and I have not done a thing, yet I know

I have accomplished more in life this morning than all the rest of my life. Let us go home for lunch." 

They arrive home. What a home of smiles and sunshine!

Lunch is served; but not much said. The young man seems to sit quietly, and as they are all expecting him to talk they too have nothing to say. 

The father asks the son if he wants to go back to the office and he says, "No, I would rather stay at home."

The smile of the mother when he says this tells him more than words could tell.

After his father leaves, his mother puts her arms around him and says, "I am so pleased. I wanted you home with me alone for awhile. Miss So and

So is going out to look after some business this afternoon. 99 When they are left alone, she goes to him and kisses him, as a proud mother should. With tears of gladness in her eyes, she tells him of her love for him and what he means to her and how she longs to go with him on his travels; but she feels his father needs her and he has someone with him who loves him too only not with a mother's love.

He smiles upon his mother, puts his arms around her and showers her with kisses. Yes, the kisses a manly boy would give to such a mother. He says to her, "I feel as though I have to give vent to the joy within so I will play you a tune upon my violin, the instrument that vibrates from the Soul." 

He takes his violin and what sweet strains pour forth from it! It carries him away into the realms of such happiness that the mother as she sits and listens could not stop her tears from flowing. He stands there seeing nothing, hearing nothing only conscious of the strains that seem to come from the very Soul. What a picture for a mother to look upon! As she was drinking, yes, drinking in these sweet strains of music, she seems to open her eyes wider, and as she looks at her boy, a light seems to formulate around him and; he bursts forth in song: 

Mother Mine, yes, Mother Mine 

I love you, yes, I do; 

And as the days go by, I know 

My love for you will never die. 

Love like this must travel on 

Yes, to the end of time 

For our love is in a Circle 

So it cannot have an end. 

Yes, a mother's love is greater 

Than all other loves combined. 

We may have a love for others; 

But it is a love of a different kind.

As he finishes his song he looks at his mother and says, "yes, I love you, Mother Dear. As I travel on my path to Wisdom, I will promise I will reach a place where no man can take my place. I hope you may live to see the day; but if you don't I know you

LOVE THROUGH MUSIC 

81 will be proud of me even on the other side for, Mother Dear, this is just the road to a life ahead." "I know it now, Son, since you have let me see the Light. What a Truth! What a clear Vision of a life worthwhile!" "Yes, Mother, you are of the Light; but the fogs of life had obscured your vision. Yet only partially as my life shows clearly that you were of the Light in the Life before this one and it was the faint memory of that life that gave you the power of penetration to see the Soul-mind within me as you did when I was but a little 'Rose Bud of Humanity.'

I owe everything I may do, everything I may know and every picture of life I may see in its Natural 

Truth to you. I cannot nor will I ever claim any of the things I do to be my own, for I get everything I have from you. You were the beginning of this life for me and you will be even unto the end, My Mother." "Son, you make me feel as if I have not lived in vain after all. Ofttimes, I have looked at myself and asked the question of myself, 'what am I? Am 

I worthy of such a Son? Who should I thank for such a gift?'" "Mother, sometime, I will show you it was not a gift. You were in the right place at the right time so here I am." He smiles but his mother does not understand. "Son, you are too deep for me.' 99 Just then his friend comes in and mother and son are alone no more that day. But, what a feeling!

Yes, a new understanding, a closer tie than ever before. Soon she will have to let him go for how long she does not know; but she is satisfied that her

son is a man and will be taken care of by the woman who has brought him through life so far. 

The rest of the week is spent by talking of their travels. They urge the parents to take at least three weeks or a month and go with them the first part of their journey. It is a fourteen day sail on the ocean and a three day trip back home on the train.

They are assured it will be a pleasant trip. At last they consent, and the day comes for their departure. 

A mother's love, a mother's pride, A joy to live with such a life

As this son-a Soul-mind pure- 

A Wisdom vast, a life supreme, A life so real, yes, not a dream.

CHAPTER XII THE COMING OUT INTO THE WORLD 

A silent night, a starry sky, A beautiful moon goes gliding by;

It makes the mind of the young man 

Expand in ecstasy sublime.

When the ship sets out to sea, a feeling of joy comes over the boy for his life is starting in real earnest. A silent secret he has had for the last two years now comes to pass and is financed by his own labor; but this does not take his Natural Self from him-it is part of his Creation even though he has created it unconsciously. How soon will he find this principle and be able to use it? He is traveling, and as this Great Book of the Creator begins to talk to him, he will soon find the principle. What a revelation it will be to him!

The first night the ocean is a wonderful sight to him. He sits upon deck with his friend beside him waiting for she knows not what. She feels she will not have to wait in vain. He is sitting quietly and looking up into the Heavens at the stars and the moon gliding over the skies and hearing the swish of the water against the sides of the ship. He sits for a long time, but at last he speaks and as he does she gets her pencil and paper ready for she sees by his face he is in touch with the vast Creation. 

When he begins to talk he says, "I never dreamed such an hour would come to me. The secret of the 

Heavens seems to reveal, and I am drifting back and back into the past. Yes, even unto the beginning of

this Earth in which we live and my Soul-mind tells me the Great Creator did not make any mysteriesonly man made mysteries; and if I go on the Path of Wisdom, the Earth will tell me from where it came, from where its waters came, how the waters. were divided from the waters, from where vegetation came, from where the beast came, how we can distinguish one species from another and how we should know what flesh to eat and what not to eat. Yes, it will tell us how the storehouse was filled and how by Evolution it was prepared for the use of man. It will tell us how the mountains were formed and why and the principle of everything will be revealed and how and from where we get our advancement. It will tells us how man came upon the Earth and what portion of man can be destroyed and what part cannot be destroyed. It will show the advancement of man and why some increased in Wisdom in the different parts of the world. It will tell us the truth of the place on the Earth man was intended to fill. It will show the trust the Creator put in man. It will show why every race of people are a different color, and have different sounds of language as we call them today. It will show us the true principle of life. It will tell us how unequality of the people came about. It will show us how the first king was put upon the throne and how it was created a hereditary Kingdom. It will show advancement of man and why man had to advance. 

It will reveal the one and only part of the Earth's treasures that does not seek the line of least resistance. Yes, the Book of Creation will reveal all things, if you seek the Path of Wisdom and realize this Path is open for anyone who may desire Wis-

dom-for what is, and not for what it can give. 

This night carries me back then brings me forward again step by step in line with the Evolutionary Periods. As I travel, all Creation of this Earth is laid bare before me. How wonderful! You know, some day, I will write a book and the name shall be 'Creation in Truth' for as I hear the Voice I know it is Truth. Why should there be any mysteries in this world of ours; when it has, by time, invention and air waves brought us close together with almost every part of the world? Do you think the people of the world today would accept 'Creation in Truth?' " "Well, if it is Truth and put in simple mental pictures as you give, why would they not accept it?” "I don't know; but as I look upon the people they seem to be traveling in some sort of groove or I might say fog. They seem to be going around in 

Circles and they are content with what they have. 

It makes me wonder, if I should shed a light on them such as I have, would they take it in the spirit given or would they rebel against me exposing my Light to them so they can see themselves in Truth in the mirror of Life? It would be a great revelation; and to some, it would be a disaster for they accumulate wealth and ease by living in the fog. They extract all the small particles of light so they, the masses, cannot see one step ahead. What a sad picture it is turning to be! Shall I go on, or do you think I should stop?' "No, go on. Your picture is not a beautiful one; but you must see the principle of darkness as well as light or you could not condemn the darkness.

The night is traveling fast; and soon will be morning; since you say you never memorize nor can you

forget we better retire for the night and continue tomorrow."

When they came on deck the next morning, our boy does not feel well. The sea is calm and the morning is warm. The fresh air breeze helps him over his sickness. As they sit on the deck, he has nothing to say but looks at the sight before him. 

The sea is so calm and blue you cannot see where the water leaves off and the horizon begins. This picture brings his young creative mind into action; but he does not tell the lesson he is getting of why the water is the same color as the sky or why the sky is the same color as the water. Here is a question to be answered. His answer is this: "The color of the sky is the reflection of the water of the sea. The depth of the water and the lack of reflection makes the sea-water appear to be blue." 

Another question comes forward-Why is this sea so calm? It seems so lazy, the ship can hardly make a ripple.

The Voice speaks to him thus: "The crust of the 

Earth is so thin between the outer and the molten heat within; and since oil is the scum produced by the molten heat, the oil penetrates through the strata and mixes with the water and makes the water so heavy that it takes so much more wind to cause it to roll than it does any other ocean-but when it does get into motion, it is so much heavier than another ocean that it hits any vessel with so much force, it is a dangerous sea in a storm. It is good there are not many wind-storms where this sea lays.' 

He sees and hears this picture illustrated. He begins to feel a little better in himself and turns and says to his parents, "I have had a wonderful revela-

tion; but it will wait until I feel more like myself. 

I think today I will sit and rest. You walk around the ship and get acquainted with the people. I see so many of them who want to be friendly with you so do not sit with me all day."

His friend is busy type-writing his conversation of the night before, therefore, stays in her stateroom all morning.

The parents see he wants to be alone; so they soon leave him with his thoughts and his sea-sickness. 

By afternoon, our boy has to go to his room and does not appear on deck again until the fifth morning. All through his sickness, he has not forgotten any of the revelation he has received when last on deck. As he sits alone, the people come toward him and want to know if he is better. He wonders how they all know him for there are many young men on board.

He asks his friend, "why do the people all come to inquire about my health? Why are they so interested in me?" "They feel your influence. They missed you the very next day when you did not appear on deck as usual. When you did not come to the dining-room, they began to inquire. You see, you are so much different than the ordinary young man, they feel your superiority." "I do not feel that way. I feel so small this morning, I wonder if it can be I" "Yes, I know, it is because you have been seasick, and you are not over it yet; but be not discouraged, you are not the only one on this ship who feels this smallness. There is a mysterious man on the ship who has been sick ever since the ship pulled into the

sea; yet, the people are saying all kinds of things about him. He looks different to the other people; and he takes his seasickness with a smile. He never leaves his stateroom; but his steward told me he thought he would be on deck today. I hope he is for the people are all saying wonderful things about him. He traveled on this ship some time ago; and some of the ship's hands tell of the wonderful things he did. They say it was really a miracle the things he did for people who were sick-not seasick—but sick and also the talks he gave at night to a circle of people."

The parents came and took their chairs beside him. 

He smiles and says, "I do believe, I am over my seasickness, and, I am thankful."

The morning is beautiful, the climate is warm and the sea is as smooth as glass. It is an ideal sight to see. As he sits in silence, his mind soon begins to receive. They are passing a mountainous country; and can see a mountain from the ship clearly. As he is looking at the beauty of the scenery, his father says, "is not that a beautiful mountain, the one alone from all the rest?"

It is surrounded by the sea. The son says, "yes, Dad, it is a beautiful mountain; but I see a more wonderful picture. I see the formation of that mountain. Listen, and, as I see, I will tell you. I see the mountain sinking, yes, sinking. It is below the water.

I see it taking the place it had years ago. The waters are calm for the mountain is there no more; but as 

I gaze below the water, I see through the strata below the ocean. Yes, and as I look, my vision carries me through into the molten heat of the Earth. 

Yes, it is far below the bottom of the sea; but is

coming up through the strata and throwing an intense heat ahead of it. It is coming nearer. I see the strata becoming hotter and hotter; then there is a splitting apart of the strata and the water is rushing into the fire through the cavity. Such a scene! 

The fire and water are at war, and-what a turmoil! 

What a whirling seething mass of fire and water! 

What a commotion! What noise! What rumbling! 

How they fight! The fire is swelling, the gasses are multiplying and there is a struggle. I see something moving, yes, the pressure of the fire, water and gasses are creating a tremendous pressure and that pressure is forcing millions of tons of the solid stratas up-up-yes, here it comes-never stopping -up through the waters of the sea; there it comes above the level of the waters. Is it never going to stop? Can you not see it, Dad? What a beautiful picture! yes, what a turmoil! It is above the sea to start, what we call, a mountain. I wonder why the mountain? The Voice says, 'you have seen the formation of the mountain in true mental pictures and you ask-why the mountain? That takes you back to the beginning of the Earth's Creation. The 

Great Creator saw, as He looked from the beginning to the end, the Principle of Creation. He knew when men came forth they would sail upon the seas for various reasons, therefore, He had a very vital mission for the mountains he formed in the seas. 

He knew men, in his first stages of sailing across the seas, would not have any power-only the winds to carry them to and fro across the sea. The wind would be their only means of power and they would be at the mercy of the winds and at times would not have power to carry the sailing vessel along. Their

journeys would often be prolonged; and a ship could only carry sufficient water for a certain length of time. Men could not drink the salt water of the seas so He, the Creator, caused the mountain to rise so as man sailed and sometimes were delayed, they could, if they discovered these mountains, steer their ship to the mountain and refresh themselves with the pure water that came from the mountain and once more fill their tanks with water and pass on their way as soon as the winds permitted.' What a wonderful thought! The Creator sees all things. There was not one step of Evolution He did not see in His Creation, and all for the benefit of His Supreme 

Creation Man." "Yes, but Son, how does this water get to the highest ridge of the mountain? Since it is in the sea, and surrounded by salt water for miles around, how is it purified and cooled?" 

He smiles as he says, "the Voice says, 'by the air pressure upon the surface of the ocean and the weight of the water itself, the water is forced through the solid strata, yes, one strata after another. As it goes through, the salt not being liquid, has to stay below while the pure clear water travels on and up to furnish a part of Creation. When it reaches a point on the mountain, it forces its way out to run in small streams down the mountain sides; and what is not needed of men goes back into its mother, the sea, again.' You see again the Circle of

Life illustrated. It is forced from the sea, travels up a hard path, flows out into the sea once more and this goes on and on that it might be ready to serve man. The Circle of Life is wonderful."

A shadow comes before them; and as the mother and son look up, they both seem to breathe deep with emotion at the same time. The shadow is a man; and as he passes them he looks at them with a smile and says, "Good Morning." 

Neither the mother or the boy can answer him for they are seeing the mental picture they have seen so many times-in the flesh once more. As this person walks down the deck, they cannot keep their eyes from him. Each knows he will come past them again. As he turns towards them again, the mother says, "yes, it must be, for no man can look like him.

He is a man alone in the world." 

Just then the man stops in front of them and with the most wonderful smile says, "we meet again." 

The mother says, "Yes." "I see you followed my instructions. What a wonderful boy you have! What a wonderful mother your boy has!" 

The boy says, “Sir, if you ever spoke truth, you spoke truth when you spoke so wonderful of my mother." "My Dear young Friend, I know when I speak truth; and since your mother is one of us and I see your father here also and your friend indeed-all

I can say is, value each one of them at their true worth for you have untold wealth in true friendship.

Keep traveling on the Path of Wisdom and life will be a life worthwhile." "You mean life can become more worthwhile than

I have found it as it is today. I have my mother, my father, a true friend and you-whoever you are; you who I have seen so many times in the spirit, heard

your voice calling me onward; you who showed me the Path of Life that means so much. Could life be more than this? If it can; and it is possible for me to attain that life, please show me the way." "Tonight, I will meet you on deck, at 8:00 

O'clock. We will have your friend with us. I see they are serving morning tea so I will go." 

The steward comes up to them, and says, "you must be proud this morning. I see the 'Man of 

Mystery' was talking to you. He has never been seen to approach anyone before, while traveling." "I wonder who he is and where he is going," the mother says.

The son-"I do not wonder who he is nor do I care where he is going; but I am longing for night to come. I am wondering what he will say tonight. 

I know, I will not be able to eat today.' 99 "I am so full of something," his friend says. 

He smiles. "I know what you are full of." 

She says, "what?" "Anticipation." "Yes, you know everything.' 

"I do not, but I hope to some day." 

Then the father says, "this is all a dream to me. 

Is it possible these things happen in real life? Do not tell me, I fell asleep and this is a dream, yet; it is too wonderful to be true." "It is so true that I know I will get tired waiting until night comes."

A general conversation prevails. 

The boy is silent for he feels he has met a Mastermind; and he is wondering what this man will talk about. Thought after thought comes to him. He smiles and says, "how good it is to be alive!"

Night draws near; and as it does it draws two 

Master-minds together the advanced and the youth. How far will the advanced take the youth upon the Path of Wisdom? These are the thoughts of his friend as she, too, dresses for the meeting at 

8:00 O'clock.

The two go on deck at the appointed time, and as they walk along, they see a lone man standing against the rail. He turns to them with "Good 

Evening," and says, "so here you are."

The young man says, "yes, I thought this day was never going to end; but here it is, and I am with it. 

Let us take our seats. It is a beautiful night. We will be comfortable." 

As they sit down, he asks the boy, "what do you think of the ocean? Has it told you its story?" "No, Sir; but I am listening for it to speak. I know its story will be both wonderful and beautiful." "Yes, but you will find sadness in it also. Everything, in Creation, has its two elements-for and against; even as our lives-only life is so much more real. We see the for and against in others; but we feel them in our own lives. The Path of Wisdom is not smooth by any means; nor is the Path of 

Wisdom easy. I know, my young friend, you will question this for so far your life has been all sunshine and your Path has been strewn with Roses.

You have been loved and cared for ever since you started your journey; but I am here to tell you, you are now on a Path and that Path leads to the masses. 

You will have to live among them, and as you travel among them, you will realize what these words mean. Before we can reach the highest planes of

life; before we can have the Wisdom of Creation; before we can have the Power of the highest gift of the Creator; before we can reach the stage where we can love humanity; before we can be sure we are on the right Path; before we can speak truth and truth only-we have to go through life in all its stages, therefore, we will have to travel on the sea of life. 

Storms shall rage and we will have to travel up steep mountains where the sides are slippery and hard to travel. We will have to cross deserts where cruel winds will blow and sand storms will overtake us. We may have to go without food and clothing for these are the symbols of the life we have to go through. As we travel on, we are lonesome and feel sometimes we are in want of a friend. Yes, as you travel on your journey through life, many temptations will beset your Path; for soon the people, as they read your books, will demand the name of its author. As soon as you give your name, you will understand the storm of the sea of which I spoke; also the cruelty of the deserts and how often you will be hungry for food of thought. The masses will confiscate so much of your time, they will put you on the point of starvation. You will have many seeking you not for yourself but because you have become famous. You will be asked to meet great men of Education in every line of thought; and they will talk to you of the things they know. It will be against your truth; but you will have to keep silent and it will be hard to do. You must not let them see how you smile, until they have finished; then speak as the Voice says you have to speak. Many will seek you, out of curiosity. Some will come with business propositions to turn your Wisdom into a

Wheel of Industry; and they will show you great possibilities and try to have you sign contracts to serve certain organizations. They will try to turn your Wisdom into Finance; but I say, listen, but do not falter from the Path you have started. Remember-He Who Creates sees the end of all things

Mortal. When Wisdom becomes a financial asset, it becomes the end of the Life of Wisdom. Many will try to tell you they, too, have the Truth and shall invite you to listen to them. The things they tell you will sound, at first, like truth; but as you listen to them, you will find, they are leading you into mysteries. As soon as you see this, be sure to say, 'no, I am not seeking mysteries, I am seeking to have all mysteries revealed.' Do not stay long with them-only long enough to say to them, “if you have one mystery in your path, you are not on the right path.' My Son of the Light, as you travel on this path, you will find many archways and at the entrance of these archways will be glaring lights as far in as you can see. Look not upon these lights too long; but look upon the man who is asking you to enter and you will see-instead, of the

Light of Life shining from his Soul-mind, you will see no light in his eyes only a deep piercing penetration as if looking beyond the lights and that is shown in his lusterless, far-reaching, searching expression of hope; yet not realized. As you turn from him, and look once more ahead of you, you see that small Circle of Light that throws light into the darkness and sends a warmth to meet you. You hear the Silent Voice say to you, 'straight ahead.' 

You can, if you want to, look upon the side light; but never lose sight of the light that bids you 'come.'

My Son of the Light, keep pressing on for the goal is worth the struggle; and as you pass along the 

Path of Wisdom you will meet men who have lived in the Light of Truth even as you have lived in the past. Advancement brings memories of the past to you in mental pictures. In time you, too, will know the people of the advanced plane, even as 

I knew you, when I first saw you. You were a mystery to the world; but to me you were an open book -as I looked through the life you should live. I saw in your mother a light also that told me she, too, is of a higher plane. I am thankful you have such a mother. Be sure to keep your eyes open, as you journey and, as you see the things that have been a mystery to the many, listen keenly, and the Voice will reveal their mysteries. There are many mys, teries, or seemingly, mysteries to the masses; but 

Creation has no mysteries, as you will see after you have traveled farther towards the Light of Truth. 

The nearer you get to the Light, the more wonderful it becomes-it shines into every speck of your life possible. It will be as a mirror so you can see your true self at all times. You will have visions, as you travel, and these visions will lead you to understand why there are so many planes for us to reach. 

You will see life illustrated in every line. When you meet the Great Master-mind, you will say, 'can I ever be as He?' You will then hear the Voice say, 'there is no limit to the heights you can reach. 

Travel on and do not falter by the wayside. Many have started; but few have reached the goal that is put before them.' My Son of the Light, I know you will come through; but you will meet many difficulties of which you do not dream. You will see the

life of 'want to' is almost past for you. You will see many things of life of which you will want to partake; and since you are growing into years in the flesh, these things will appeal to you. Do not forget you are in a new Path and this Path is 'do I need to' not 'want to.' You will have to fight hard to win the victory over your Human Natural Self; but weigh everything that comes before you carefully and say to yourself, 'is it worthwhile? Will this retard my progress? I am a man in the world of the few, would these few let me do as the masses?' I think your Soul-mind will say, 'no, the sacrifice is too great, keep on the Path of Wisdom.' Now, My Young 

Friend, I am pleased to have had this night with you. Remember, if, at any time, you need a friend, you can always call upon me-no matter where you are or where I am-distance means nothing to he who has found the principle of Creation and knows consciously how to use it. If you have enjoyed this night as much as I, the enjoyment is mutual. I bid you, 'Good Night, and may you ever keep in line with the Light of Truth.""

As he leaves, the boy heaves a sigh. He feels he can not expand enough to satisfy himself. He feels so full of an inward peace, an inward joy, a running stream of happiness. His face can be seen in the moonlight; and it shines with a Light of Peace such as it never has shown before. He has met and heard the Great Man of Wisdom talk. To think-he is the man who had appeared to him so many times in his life as he dwelt upon the mountain. He has often seen this man standing beside him with a smile of encouragement; and he seems to hear him say, "come, I am with thee. Never falter for your life

is real-a reality of which you could not dream."

Life seems to me just an unwinding of something; and as it unwinds, it shows life after life more wonderful. Such a picture! A never-ending picture! 

You look forward from day to day, so you might live to improve its beauty. A picture you will never finish; but carry it through the ages from life to life -the new life beginning where the old life left off —an ever-increasing beautiful memory. Life must be eternal for man has so much to see; so much to know; so much to do to reach the place where his 

Creator saw him-he must live on and on to do this. How wonderful to know there is no ending to such a beautiful Soul! It can, if it will, keep accumulating Wisdom and more Wisdom, Power and more Power, Love and more Love, a greater understanding of the meaning of these words as time goes on. What a Path! What a goal! What a reward awaiting us at every new plane we reach! These thoughts drift through the mind of our young man. 

Are they not thoughts that would make his very 

Soul swell with joy and the light of his eyes sparkle with a true natural smile that shows his beauty in the moonlight?

He starts to tell his friend a story of a vision he thought was a dream. Since his friend of Wisdom had told him he would have visions, he remembers this vision that stands before him very vividly: "Long ago, yes, I cannot tell how far back in my life; but I lived as the masses. I was one of them; and the fog of life surrounded me. I felt chilled with its dampness, and was so miserable and discontented with life that I felt like giving up. The thought came to me, 'can you be such a coward?' With this

thought, I felt something arise within me and say, 'no, I am not a coward; but this life does not satisfy me. There must be something somewhere worth the living; and if there is I must find it. The Creator surely did not put man upon the Earth to be as small as I am tonight. I will fight on and win. I know there must be something for which to live, if I can only find it. I have tried everything I know to lift myself out of this useless life I feel I am living. 

What can I do? I am so discontented with this life.'

With these thoughts running through my mind, I felt the presence of someone near me. I turned and there stood a man. He smiled and said, 'you are not satisfied with the life you are living and you wish to see life that is worth living? Follow me, and I will show you the Path that leads to the life worthwhile.' I did not hesitate for I was ready for anything that would take me out of the fog I had been living. He led me to a path that ascended up the mountain. It was steep and straight and was hard to travel. I had gone a short distance when I began to falter. It was hard to keep going; yet, when I looked up to him, he seemed to make it easy. The thought came, 'if he can so can I.' I then started with a new vigor. When he saw me stop, he did not say a word; but smiled upon me as if to say, 'surely you can be as big a man as I. No man would give up, if he be a man, once he starts to climb.' This made me anxious to show him, although I had lived in the fog, I still had a speck of manhood left. I again doubled my efforts and followed on; but soon found the going was easier. It was the beginning of the Path that was hard to travel. At last, we reached a plane and as I stepped upon it, he smiled

and said, 'you have done well. You must be anxious to live, since you have traveled this path as you have. 

I have led many to it; but few have reached where you now stand. You shall have your reward-Look through these glasses of Creation; and you will behold the most beautiful scenery of the Creator's Creation. After you have feasted upon the life of beauty, look down upon the dampness and fog you left behind. Then tell me if it is worth the struggle.' 

I looked down and saw from where I came; and how small an area my vision had been compared to what I could see through the eyes of Creation. I thought: 'I am glad I became discontented and did not give up.' What a wonderful thing it is to meet a friend such as I had found! I feasted upon the beauties of the scenery and drank in the pure air; and was swelling with emotional joy when my friend spoke again and said, 'this is just the first plane above the masses. The mist sometimes reaches this plane; and as they do they bring coldness and dampness with them and envelope those who are content to stay here. Then again they doubt and fear to be alone. In the darkness of the fog, they fall down again into the fog below. Beyond this, there is another plane. Will you want to follow me?' I will; since I have seen this much of the Light and feel it, if there is more light-lead the way, I will follow, no matter how hard the path. To my surprise, the path was not so hard for the light of the first plane seemed to connect with the second plane; and I could see where to put my feet. I changed both in body and mind. My body was healthier, my mind worked easier and the thoughts that came to me were getting more manly. I looked up the path and

there was my friend again—smiling upon me, and as I smiled up to him, he said, 'He who seeks the Light shall find it; then the Light will lead him on. Come, my friend, you are doing well.' As he said these words, I felt something fall from me. I knew I was finding something I had been longing for for years past-and this something that fell from me was the shackles of Circumstances that keep the masses down. I felt free, yes, free and light. I knew I had been born again—this time into a life worthwhile. 

As I put my feet upon the second plane, the air was so pure, the mountain plane so strong that I felt a sort of vibration go through me. My friend spoke again, 'now, look through the eyes of Creation and you will see further; you will see more and the picture will be more beautiful-so much more beautiful than the first plane.' I looked and drank in this wonderful vast scenery. I felt as if I should say something to express myself; but, try as I would, I could not find words big enough. I sighed; and these words came to me-'What a creation! What a 

Creator!' Can you remember when first I used those words? It was after the vision I, from the second plane, saw Creation and the name Creator was then given to me as the Great Central Power of the Universe. The word Creator means so much; yet, as you let your mind expand and expand and try to realize What or Who this Creator is-it leads you to a Path without end; a Path that has no material wish; a height without limit; a depth that the mind of man can never fathom. All we can say is, how vast a Creation; how great a Creator; then we have said all man is big enough to say-to say more is 

Idle Words. To try to explain is foolish. To listen.

to anyone try to explain is-where two fools meet; so I say, and this is as far as I can go-'I see His Creation; I feel that something that cannot be spoken; I know there is a power He Created that we could use, and that power is man's ability to use Creation to the capacity of his Wisdom; I listen to 

His Creation and it speaks to me of its own free will and the stories it tells are all Truth. The pictures 

Creation paints are unsurpassable-even as the sunset in the Heavens, the moon gliding through the sky, the universe studded with the twinkling stars, the hills, the mountains, the rivers, the seas, the ever changing color of the seasons, the many varieties of the shades of the trees and all other pictures painted by Time and Evolution. How can man ever be big enough or see a true mental picture that he might paint as nature does?' These thoughts ran through my mind; and I looked upon my friend -he looked so clean, so manly, his eyes shone with a beautiful light and his smile was good to look upon. He said, 'I will leave you here for a while.

Look, listen and many things will be shown thee. 

Some day we will meet again. I hope, by your own strength, you may reach the third plane. The victory will then be great indeed.' I said, 'since you have shown me the Path this far, I will never turn back. I will go on, no matter what or how hard the 

Path may be.' He said, 'you speak well. I will meet you again.' This vision has come back so strong. I wonder if this man, who talked to us tonight, can be the same friend of my vision? I wonder?" 

She says, "to me, you are all wonder. The more 

I listen to you, the more you make me wonder if this is real and not just a dream. I know I am on

a ship, I can hear and feel the vibration of the engine, I can hear the swish of the water; but you carry me away so far from the Earth that I wonder if I will ever find myself again. I think I, too, have been born again for I am living in an entirely new world; a new vision of real life and I can almost see beyond this life to the life that has to be. What a secret revealed when you know Wisdom is so vast -and we were told by the Creator that we could have Wisdom without limit-that makes it so clear that we must live on and on. Where could there be a man who could amass all this Wisdom in one short life-this Wisdom so vast it has no boundary-the 

Universe may; but Wisdom, no. What an incentive to live on and on, constantly seeking Wisdom; 

Wisdom-There is no other word to mean so much and there is only one other word greater—and that is Creator. And this Creator is wonderful. Only those who can stand upon the mountain of Wisdom and look with the eyes of Wisdom and have the understanding of the word Wisdom can see the the Creator in His greatness and know what He meant when He said as He saw this Earth complete -'And I looked upon my work, and it was good.' 

When will this time be?" "You surely are advancing," he said, "not in a dream; but into real life. If you go on, you will have to write-not me." "Do you know the time?" "Yes, 2 o'clock." "Do you not think this is enough for one night?" "Yes, but I do not feel tired, I am happy tonight." "Then let us go to our rooms and lay down in

To meet a friend so big in life 

That his Wisdom carries you on; 

And makes you swear that if— 

Ever man can reach such height; 

Then I will be that one.

Next morning, as they all came on deck, he asks his parents how they are enjoying the trip. "It is wonderful," the mother said, "the sea, the sky and the people are wonderful. I never knew people could be so interesting; everyone seems to become one family. It surely is a revelation after living in the cities to know how people mingle and talk when away from home. They are all guessing who the author can be. Some say, 'it must be a man with wonderful experience in life, and a complete knowledge of the Earth.' They comment on his using the word Creator and say 'it is the most wonderful book-so new, yet so natural and easy to understand!' 

"That is enough, mother. I do not want to know what the people are saying about me." "How can a mother of a son like you keep everything to herself? You have to open the safety valves sometimes." 

He smiles, as he says, "yes, I guess so."

He turns to his father, "how are you enjoying your vacation ?" "Well, Son, if I get started, I will have more to say than your mother-so I will just say, I am satisfied-more than I could tell. CHAPTER XIV THE FIRST GLIMPSE INTO THE DARKNESS OF LIFE 

Life for some seems a pleasure, To others it is just sad. 

Yet through the cloud of darkness, A kind word may make someone glad. "Let us take a walk around the ship, you have not been able to see much of the ship or the passengers. We shall spend the afternoon sight-seeing and character-reading. We shall exchange our opinions of the different passengers that may interest us. I think we can, in that way, spend a pleasant afternoon"-so they start their afternoon walk and we will leave them until evening.

After dinner our young man comes on deck and sits in his chair. He enjoys the peace and quiet of the ship. He is thinking what a wonderful life this is; when there passes him a person whom he has noticed many times before. He says to himself, "The restless man-why can a man be as this man seems to be on such a ship, such a night and such a beautiful sky to look upon." 

The man stops before him and says, "Why do you smile all the time? Can life be so pleasant that you can sit there and smile? Young man, I do not like your smile-it has haunted me; and I have made up my mind to tell you there is nothing for which to smile-and as I tell you my story, I think 

I will see a change come over you. 99 They mysterious man, as he is called by the people

GLIMPSE INTO DARKNESS OF LIFE 111 

on the ship, comes towards them and says, "May I sit down?" "Yes, Sir. This other gentleman thinks that life has no smiles; and he was just going to tell me why -as you came up." "May I listen, I know I will be interested; for to me this world has smiles; but it has its sadnesses too -so I would like to hear the story of your life, Sir." "I shall be pleased to have you listen. It is a long story but true; and in it there is love and tragedy." "Well, I am sure it will be interesting," said the mysterious man; "but be sure and start at the beginning so we will get a true picture of what your life has been, what it is and what it should be." "Here it is: I am anxious to tell it to someone,

I have kept it so long to myself it almost unmans me; but, as I said, I will tell it just as it happened; 

I was born to a wonderful mother and father of whom I was proud. I was brought up to look up to an Ideal of a man who was a man of men. I was first taken to church, then I went of my own accord; and as time went on, I became a teacher in the Sunday School even keeping myself clean both in habits and mind. When I was twenty-eight years of age, I felt lonesome and I did not know what this meant for I was really busy all the time. Yet this loneliness kept creeping over me. Women did not mean much in my life, I did not seem to have time to think as other men; but one day, I was sitting in my office when a mother and her daughter came in-the mother came up to me and told me their business and I was interested in business only; but as she ceased talking, I looked up and my eyes met the eyes

of this young woman. I knew or seemed to feel, in a moment, why I had been so lonesome; and I knew that this woman was the other part of me. She never spoke. I could have finished my work for them right then; but I did not. I drifted into conversation with the mother. As long as the girl was so near, I felt sort of complete-more complete than I had ever been before. I said, 'you better come back tomorrow.' They, too, seemed to be satisfied. I thought tomorrow would never come. 

Being a man of, what I thought, Truth, that night I thanked God for bringing this someone into my life. 

The next day did come; and I made sure no one else would come that afternoon-so I could keep them as long as possible. When they were shown into my office, the sun shone with a new light; and as I looked upon her, I knew I had found the other part of myself. How I loved her! I found out later, she felt just the same as I did. Such love! Time went on; but time meant nothing to me: I lived in the land of love, rain or shine, cold or warm, night or day made no difference to me. I had a love that kept me in the life of pleasantness always. We set the wedding day-then another eternity to wait.

I thought it would never come; but as everything else in life came, it came, and by the laws we became as one just as we had been by the laws of love. We went to our beautiful home which I had built to be our earthly paradise. I shall never forget the day

I took her home, yes, home, how we lived together! 

What a life! What a beautiful life! We were as one in the church, and gave thanks unto our God as well as a tenth of my income. We lived in a land of love and contentment; but one day when I

GLIMPSE INTO DARKNESS OF LIFE 113 

came home from my work, she whispered something to me. How glad I was! No man can understand the thrill I got; but as the days passed, I saw my love was not so strong as she used to be and this worried me. I began to watch her; then one day when I came home, I found her in bed. I went in to see her; and she said, 'I felt so weak, I just had to lie down; but it will soon pass off.' The next day, she did not get out of bed so I sent for a doctor. 

He said she had a deep set cold on her chest, and her condition was against her. He seemed to make no headway. I called in another Doctor, then another, but they could do nothing. I thought of this 

Great and Wonderful God; and made up my mind, if the Doctors failed, my prayers would be answered. 

I began to pray and pray-How I told my God the good she had done, what she meant to me, how I could not nor would not live without her if he would only see fit to restore her back to health, how I would worship and sing His praises and thank Him as long as life lasted! How I prayed! Day by day, she continued to slip from me. I went to our minister and asked his help; and we both knelt down and prayed, but, no, she was getting weaker. I was getting desperate; so I went to the minister again and asked him what we could do. We decided we would have all the members pray and send up their petition to God at the same time. They did—but, no, their prayers were not answered. The day came when she called me to her and said, 'we must part. 

I cannot stay with you much longer; but let us be brave and face the parting as man and woman should. I know you will be lonesome; but I will be lonesome too. Hold on to life: Life is wonderful!

If I could only live, for your sake. I feel my body getting weak and I am so tired.' As I sat and listened to her, a feeling came over me; and I took her in my arms and held her to me and said, 'can it be possible, the God I serve can look down upon us?

I need you so much, you want to live and have we not been taught that if we pray and have faith that He would answer our prayers.' Even as I talked, I felt her slip away from me; but just before she left she smiled and said, 'I will see you again, Good 

Bye.' She was gone. All I had in my arms was the lifeless body of my wonderful wife. For three days, I lived in a haze, I knew nothing that was going on, life seemed to be sort of blank or mechanical: people asked me questions, I do not remember how I answered them. The day of the final parting came and they put her body in the last resting place. 

I felt a damp chill pass over me. When the preacher talked at the grave-side, I wanted to scream and tell him 'it was all mockery': there was no God, there was no Truth, there was nothing Good. When I got home, home? no, hell, I sat until everyone left; then I burst forth and cursed the God that gave us life I cursed him for the love He had given me, nay, I denied and still deny a God, I believe in nothing the sun does not shine, it just maddens me, the strains of music pretty nearly drives me out of my mind, to hear someone laugh takes me through the torture of hell-life, what is life?-Hell, Hell, Hell." "Wait a while, Sir." "No, let me go on and finish my story. I must finish it, I have wanted to tell it to someone ever

since it happened; but no one will listen to me. Let me, Sir, finish my story." "All right, go on." "I was going to say, I left the world, I could not bear to hear laughter, I could not stand to see lovers together, I could not bear to hear preachers try to tell the people of prayers and faith. I just had to get away from everything and everybody. I am not fit to mingle with the people, my life is just one straight line of curses, yes, as I once prayed, now I curse; and as long as I live, I will never, I know, be fit to intermingle with the people. Oh, how I hate myself! Oh, how I hate everyone! What a false life! What teachings! I have to laugh, when

There have been thousands who have lived as you lived, loved as you loved, prayed as you prayed and then not seeing their prayers answered, cursed as you have cursed and gone through hell just as you have; all because of a misunderstanding of the true position of man. Now, my friend, I do not find fault with the feelings you have towards the Creator-it is not your fault. As we are taught so we are. As we accept the teachings given us so shall our lives be molded. There are some who refuse to be molded; and it is these who seek after truth and seek for themselves; when they seek, they find that the teachings are not what the Creator meant them to be. 

I have lived long and watched wisely the people; and through this searching, I found the Truth of Life. It brings thoughts that if spoken to the masses they would first be shocked and even call you an Atheist; but No, it does not lead to Atheism but true life. and gave credit to the church. Then trials came your way. You were taught to put your trust in your God whom you had lived to please; yet when the crisis of your life came and you put your trust in

Why?-Well, I will tell you Truth and nothing but Truth; then you will see just where you stand today: When man came forth, the Creator, gave him first a free will, a mind with three parts. 

On one side, He told man there was no limit to the height he could go in beautiful thoughts; on the other side he could go down, yes, down lower than the animal-but in between He put a free will and said, 'as thou thinkest so thou shall be.' Creation, therefore the body dies and goes back from whence it came, but the Soul-mind lives on forever. When He gave the body the Soul-mind, it was then the organs of the body became the servant of the brain power. take came in-He did not take her from you or He did not allow it to be. Man might be small enough to do that, but not this Great and Wonderful Creator; so you see, He had no hand in the death of your loved one; she caught a cold, disease got the better of her, the flesh was too weak to resist the disease, therefore she succumbed to disease-not the will of God. He does not show interference in anyone's life in any way, shape or form. He gave you a trust even as He gave every 

Soul-mind He created a trust; and that trust is not of the flesh but of the Soul-mind only. The weakness of the flesh has nothing to do with the Creator's part of man. If the Doctors had been big enough to counteract the disease, she would not have died; so why should you curse the Creator for a thing He had no right to interfere with; why should you curse the light of the beautiful sun; why should the laughter of others grate upon your nerves or the smile of this boy agitate you? Do you not think it is these things happening that brings us into a life to be analyzed in its true sense? Let me tell you, I think you are a conscientious man or you would not have taken your trial so hard. You lived in your christian faith in earnest, you loved in earnest, you are just as earnest in the thoughts you have today and you have a right to be thus; but you must know the truth: 

You alone are the victim of your own circumstances. 

You alone have the right to see as you see today, to

think as you think today; but remember you are the only victim. It matters not to the Creator how you curse Him, He never hears you. It matters not how the laughter of others affect you, they still laugh on.

Do you not know that the mills of time grind slowly but surely, and each one of us seems to get into this grinding sometime in life;-and if you are man enough to stand it and say to yourself: 'I do not care how hard it grinds even if they put spikes in the rollers to hurt me more, I will stand it like a man, I will not be a coward and give up but will let it grind for the more the grinding the harder the fight, the harder the fight the bigger the victory, the greater the victory the more manly will I be when the wheels of time cease to grind me.' After this grinding, your depressed Soulmind awakens to the weakness you have displayed you will feel ashamed of yourself and as you look into your mirror you can say, "Once I saw a man's reflection; but do I see a man today?' Have you had a dream or seen your loved one in your sleep? If you have, did she look satisfied? I ask these questions-so I will understand your true standing in Soul-mind to Soul-mind; for such a love as you and she seemed to have could not have been of the flesh only-it must have been a Soul love, and you know, the Soul-mind love is in a Circle; and this Circle can not be broken. You may be parted for a time, and there may be others come into each of your lives that seem to be love; but there is only one 

Soul-mind who can be the true Soul-mate, and sooner or later, as time goes on, they will meet and understand and will utter words such as these: "This seems different.' It is something you cannot find words to express, nor would you want to, you just want to be content to feel that something that you know is not of the flesh but the true gift of the Creator in the beginning and will go on even unto the end. The two Souls put together shall become in the end even as at the beginning-two-Souls-inone. The blending was beautiful, My Son, yes I am old enough to call you, Son. I want you to see the truth for I know, once you see the Light, and are put on the Path of Wisdom, what a new life you will and can have! The Path of Wisdom is easy.

First, know that the Creator gave you a Soul-mind with a promise that there is no limit to the mind

even unto Creation itself; then He said 'Seek Wisdom and all Truth shall be revealed unto thee. The
